    // IntersectionObserver has partial support in chrome <= 57 (https://caniuse.com/#feat=intersectionobserver)
    var supportIntersectionObserver = 'IntersectionObserver' in window && 'IntersectionObserverEntry' in window && 'isIntersecting' in window.IntersectionObserverEntry.prototype;

  function mergeOptions(target, source) {
    var list = [target, source];
    var cloned = {};

    for (var i = 0; i <= list.length; i++) {
      if (typeof list[i] === 'object' && list[i]) {
        for (var prop in list[i]) {
          cloned[prop] = list[i][prop];
        }
      }
    }
    return cloned;
  }

  function imageLazyLoading(imageLoadClassName, options) {
        if (!imageLoadClassName && options && options.loadMode === 'onload') {
          imageLoadClassName = 'onload-loadable';
        }
        imageLoadClassName = imageLoadClassName || defaults.imageLoadClassName;

        options = mergeOptions(defaults.options, options);

        var lazyImages = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.' + imageLoadClassName));

        function loadImages(img) {
            function setSource(attr, srcKey) {
                if (img.hasAttribute(srcKey)) {
                    var dataSrc = img.getAttribute(srcKey);
                    img.setAttribute(attr, dataSrc);
                    img.removeAttribute(srcKey);
                }
            }

            setSource('src', dataSrcKey);
            setSource('srcset', dataSrcSetKey);

            img.classList.remove(imageLoadClassName);

            if (img.classList.length < 1) {
                img.removeAttribute('class');
            }
        }

        function loadAllImages() {
            lazyImages.forEach(function(lazyImage) {
                loadImages(lazyImage);
            });
        }

        if (supportIntersectionObserver && options.loadMode === 'lazy') {
            var lazyImageObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries, observer) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                    var lazyImage = entry.target;
                    loadImages(lazyImage);
                    lazyImageObserver.unobserve(lazyImage);
                }
                });
            }, options);

            lazyImages.forEach(function(lazyImage) {
                lazyImageObserver.observe(lazyImage);
            });

            window.lazyImageObserver = lazyImageObserver;

        } else {
            if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
                loadAllImages();
            } else {
                window.addEventListener('load', function() {
                    loadAllImages();
                });
            }
        }
    }

      imageLazyLoading();

        function getSubdomain() {
        var dom = "" + window.location.host;
        var pos = dom.indexOf("mercadoli");
        if (pos == -1)
          pos = dom.indexOf("deremate");
        if (pos == -1)
          pos = dom.indexOf("arremate");
        if (pos != -1)
          dom = dom.substring(pos);
        if (dom.indexOf(":") >= 0) {
          dom = dom.substring(0, dom.indexOf(":"));
        }

        return dom;
      }

      function setCookie(cookieName, cookieValue, nDays) {

        var domain = getSubdomain();
        var path = "/";

        if (nDays != null) {

          today = new Date();

          expire = new Date();
          if (nDays == null || nDays == 0)
            nDays = 1;
          expire.setTime(today.getTime() + 3600000 * 24 * nDays);

          document.cookie = cookieName + "=" + cookieValue + ";path=" + path + ";domain=." + domain + ";expires=" + expire.toGMTString();
        } else {
          document.cookie = cookieName + "=" + cookieValue + ";path=" + path + ";domain=." + domain;
        }
      }


      function performNaturalCheck(referrer, currentUrl, word) {
        if (!word) {
          return;
        }

        ind = getNaturalReferrerInd(referrer, currentUrl);

        if (ind == -1) {
          return;
        }
        naturalPmsSiteId = naturalPmsSiteIds[ind];

      }

      function _getUrlSubdomain(url) {
        var from = url.indexOf(".");
        var to = url.indexOf("/", from + 1);
        if (to == -1) {
          to = url.length;
        }
        return url.substring(from + 1, to);
      }

      function getCookieValueCall1(name) {
        var start = document.cookie.indexOf(name + "=");
        var len = start + name.length + 1;
        if (start == -1) return null;
        var end = document.cookie.indexOf(";", len);
        if (end == -1) end = document.cookie.length;
        return unescape(document.cookie.substring(len, end));
      }

      function getNaturalReferrerInd(referrer, currentUrl) {
        fromPms = getCookieValueCall1("pmsonline");
        //document.write("<BR>pmsonline:"+fromPms+"<BR>");
        if (fromPms != null) {
          if (fromPms == "YES") {
            setCookie("pmsonline", currentUrl, null);
            return -1;
          }
          if (fromPms.toLowerCase() == currentUrl.toLowerCase())
            return -1;

        iQueryIndex = referrer.indexOf("?");
        if (iQueryIndex != -1)

          if (referrer.indexOf(naturalSites[i]) != -1)
            finded = i;
        }
        return finded;
      }
